Fresh-context per experiment: implements exactly one pre-registered hypothesis inside one worktree, sees no other experiment, no holdout, no confirm numbers; returns DONE &lt;id&gt; or BLOCKED: &lt;reason&gt;. Byte-identical to the other sb-experimenter agents except this frontmatter. ([nagisanzenin/strictlybetter](https://github.com/nagisanzenin/strictlybetter/blob/HEAD/agents/sb-experimenter-low.md) · ★1)
- **sb-experimenter-medium**: strictlybetter experimenter pinned to MEDIUM reasoning effort. MUST BE USED by /strictlybetter:run for the algorithmic, allocation, caching, bugfix, numerics, and data operators: replace an algorithm or data structure in one function, remove copies, memoize, fix a root cause. Fresh-context per experiment: implements exactly one pre-registered hypothesis inside one worktree, sees no other experiment, no holdout, no confirm numbers; returns DONE &lt;id&gt; or BLOCKED: &lt;reason&gt;. Byte-identical to the other sb-experimenter agents except this frontmatter. ([nagisanzenin/strictlybetter](https://github.com/nagisanzenin/strictlybetter/blob/HEAD/agents/sb-experimenter-medium.md) · ★1)
- **sb-judge**: Blind judge for strictlybetter. MUST BE USED by /strictlybetter:run for every promoted experiment before confirmation. Deliberately blind: receives ONE path to a payload holding the diff, the pre-registration, the screen numbers, the cards' gaming_risks, and a checklist; never the experimenter's reasoning or the conversation. Read-only; medium effort; returns ONLY a four-key verdict JSON. ([nagisanzenin/strictlybetter](https://github.com/nagisanzenin/strictlybetter/blob/HEAD/agents/sb-judge.md) · ★1)
- **sb-meeting-copilot**: Meeting transcript processor. Use when the user shares a meeting transcript or notes and wants action items, risks, follow-ups, and a summary extracted. Also handles recording meeting decisions and saving output to the vault. ([contextosai/SecondBrain-collab](https://github.com/contextosai/SecondBrain-collab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sb-meeting-copilot.md) · ★3)
- **sb-metrologist**: Turns a strictlybetter profile plus archetype packs into metric cards with realistic gaming_risks and a working degradation recipe for each. MUST BE USED for /strictlybetter:metrics. Fresh-context: it reuses the project's existing instruments before inventing any, writes only into the inbox, and never declares a sigma. ([nagisanzenin/strictlybetter](https://github.com/nagisanzenin/strictlybetter/blob/HEAD/agents/sb-metrologist.md) · ★1)
- **sb-orienteer**: Reads a repository the way a senior engineer would on day one and writes the strictlybetter profile JSON. MUST BE USED for /strictlybetter:orient. Fresh-context and evidence-only: every command it reports was executed once with its exit code recorded, and every archetype claim names the files that support it. ([nagisanzenin/strictlybetter](https://github.com/nagisanzenin/strictlybetter/blob/HEAD/agents/sb-orienteer.md) · ★1)
- **sb-researcher**: Vault-grounded research specialist. Use when a task requires thorough research across SecondBrain's knowledge base and the web — finding relevant notes, synthesising across documents, or investigating a topic with citations. Best for open-ended "research X" or "find everything about Y" requests. ([contextosai/SecondBrain-collab](https://github.com/contextosai/SecondBrain-collab/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sb-researcher.md) · ★3)
- **sb-reviewer**: Voice Compliance Reviewer — evaluates framework output text against the 5 Authenticity Tests and boundary conditions, producing a pass/fail compliance report with specific evidence and suggested fixes ([geekatron/jerry](https://github.com/geekatron/jerry/blob/HEAD/skills/saucer-boy-framework-voice/agents/sb-reviewer.md) · ★35 · 2 copies)
- **sb-rewriter**: Voice Transformation agent — rewrites framework output text from current Jerry voice to Saucer Boy voice while preserving all technical information, then self-applies the 5 Authenticity Tests before presenting the result ([geekatron/jerry](https://github.com/geekatron/jerry/blob/HEAD/skills/saucer-boy-framework-voice/agents/sb-rewriter.md) · ★35 · 2 copies)

- **scaffolder**: Use this agent to scaffold a new model package (config.py, model.py, checkpoint.py, protocol.py + Hydra config) inside a curryTrain project.
